# itc Professional Sound — Technical Specifications (all products)

> Companion to itc-prosound-catalogue.md. One section per product: description, highlights, full parameter table.

## Amplifiers

### 2 Channel Professional Amplifier TC-2200B

*DSP Series Amplifiers* · AVN page: https://www.audiovisualnepal.com/products/amplifiers/itc-tc-2200b · Spec sheet: https://www.audiovisualnepal.com/downloads/itc/itc-tc-2200b-spec-sheet.pdf

The itc TC-2200B is a 2 Channel Professional Amplifier from itc's DSP Series Amplifiers range, built for powering loudspeaker systems in halls, auditoriums, places of worship, banquet venues and live events.
Key figures: output power Stereo@8Ω: 200W×2 Stereo@4Ω: 400W×2; frequency response 20Hz-20KHz/±1dB @8Ω; thd+n ≤0.01%. Highlights include built-in DSP processor, optional control methods, independent adjustment of microphone and music and built-in 48V phantom switch control function.
AudioVisual Nepal supplies, installs and commissions itc professional sound systems across Nepal — with system design, acoustic simulation on request, and local after-sales support.

**Features**

- **Built-in DSP processor** — Realize various scene presets Sound processing functions such as reverb, echo, howling, noise cancellation, aviation, equalization, and pitch shifting
- **Optional control methods** — IRremote control Coding switch Button control
- **Independent adjustment of microphone and music** — Volume adjustment Effect adjustment Treble/alto/bass adjustment Input gain adjustment
- **Built-in 48V phantom switch control function**
- **Comprehensive protective measures** — Soft start protection Overvoltage protection Undervoltage protection Overload protection Short circuit protection Overheat protection

| Group | Parameter | Value |
|---|---|---|
| Output | Output power | Stereo@8Ω: 200W×2 Stereo@4Ω: 400W×2 |
| Performance | Input sensitivity | 2.2dBu(1V) |
| Connectivity | Input impedance | 10KΩ |
| Output | Frequency response (@1W Power) | 20Hz-20KHz/±1dB @8Ω |
| Output | THD+N (@1/8 power) | ≤0.01% |
| General | Isolation (@1KHz) | ≥80dB |
| Performance | Damping coefficient(@1KHz) | ≥200@ 8 ohms |
| Performance | SNR (A-weighted) | ≥93dB |
| Connectivity | Input voltage | ~220V/50Hz |
| Output | Overall power consumption | 150W |
| Physical | Dimension (L*W*H) | 484*206*44 |
| Physical | Weight | 3.3kg |

**Applications:** Events, Religious, Education, Hotel, Government

### Professional Digital Amplifier KT-P21000

*KT Series Amplifiers* · AVN page: https://www.audiovisualnepal.com/products/amplifiers/itc-kt-p21000 · Spec sheet: https://www.audiovisualnepal.com/downloads/itc/itc-kt-p21000-spec-sheet.pdf

The itc KT-P21000 is a professional Digital Amplifier from itc's KT Series Amplifiers range, built for powering loudspeaker systems in halls, auditoriums, places of worship, banquet venues and live events.
Key figures: output power Continuous power amplification; frequency response 20Hz~20kHz/+3~-1dB; thd+n ≤0.01%. Highlights include more than 95% ultra-high efficiency and excellent stability, with signal, power, temperature voltage limit function, MONO/STEREO/BRIDGE mode for selection and temperature-controlled fan.
AudioVisual Nepal supplies, installs and commissions itc professional sound systems across Nepal — with system design, acoustic simulation on request, and local after-sales support.

**Features**

- **More than 95% ultra-high efficiency and excellent stability** — Variable oscillation modulation technology Multiple feedback regulation technology Innovative output power control technology
- **With signal, power, temperature voltage limit function**
- **MONO/STEREO/BRIDGE mode for selection**
- **Temperature-controlled fan** — Turn on immediately when booting Accelerate as the temperature rises Full speed at about 60℃
- **Normal loads of 8 ohms, 4 ohms, and 2 ohms**
- **Comprehensive protective measures** — Overvoltage protection Undervoltage protection Overcurrent protection DC protection Short circuit protection

|---|---|---|

The itc TS-12PFX is a mixer from itc's Analog Mixers with DSP range, built for front-of-house and installation mixing in halls, churches, temples, schools and live events.
Key figures: phantom power +48V with switch; power supply External AC18V/1A（AC 230-240V 50/60Hz）power adapter; power consumption ≤18W. - **3-band EQ adjustment for each mono input channel**
- **Output channel** — 1 stereo main output 1 auxiliary output 1 stereo monitor output 1 earphone monitor output 1 CD/Tape output
- **Built-in 24-bit DSP audio processor** — Includevocal, small house, hall, echo, echo + reverberation, plate, chorus GTR, rotating GTR, vibrato GTR type Provide100 preset effects

Key figures: frequency response 50Hz-15kHz; sensitivity (-49±2dB)(at  1KHz)(0dB=1V/Pa); normal impedance 600Ω. Highlights include perfect sound quality, hypercardioid technology design, excellent feedback suppression system and high-quality magnetic circuit design.

- **Perfect sound quality** — Thick low frequency Rich medium and high frequency Penetrating sound quality Wide sound range
- **Hypercardioid technology design** — Ensure the frequency sound gap between the main axis and the off-axis
- **Excellent feedback suppression system** — Ensure high-level output even in high sound pressure environment
- **High-quality magnetic circuit design** — Improve output sensitivity
- **Strong resistance to water and corrosion** — Strong zinc alloy structure Flexible steel nets with non-deformation Special plating or baking paint technology
